# Examen jefe — Certificación de Inglés

> Logro #41 (certificación C1; formato de referencia: Cambridge C1 Advanced). Pool de **500 preguntas** en 5 secciones; cada intento sortea 100 respetando la proporción 30 gramática / 25 lectura / 20 audición / 15 escritura / 10 oral. Sin teoría asociada: sólo cuestionario. Escrito directamente para el examen (no reutiliza los cuestionarios por tema).

---

## Sección: gramatica — Vocabulario y gramática (Use of English) (150 preguntas)

```
metadata:
  materia: "idiomas-extranjeros/ingles"
  tema: "examen-jefe"
  nivel: "C1"
  tags: ["gramatica", "examen-jefe"]
pasos:
  - "Leer con atención y elegir la opción correcta."
enunciado: "Not until the report was published ＿＿ the extent of the damage."
opciones_explicitas:
  - "did we realise"
  - "we realised"
  - "we did realise"
  - "realised we"
respuesta: "did we realise"
tipo: mc
explicacion: "Tras 'Not until' al inicio se invierte auxiliar + sujeto: did we realise."
```

## Sección: reading — Lectura (125 preguntas)

```
metadata:
  materia: "idiomas-extranjeros/ingles"
  tema: "examen-jefe"
  nivel: "C1"
  tags: ["reading", "examen-jefe"]
pasos:
  - "Leer con atención y elegir la opción correcta."
enunciado: "Texto: «Remote work was long dismissed by managers as a perk for the trusted few. The pandemic forced a vast experiment, and early data suggested that output held steady or even rose. Yet several studies now show a subtler picture: individual tasks improve at home, while spontaneous collaboration, which drives innovation, quietly declines. Companies that mandate a full return to the office risk resentment; those that abandon it entirely risk losing the shared culture that binds teams together.» What is the writer's main point?"
opciones_explicitas:
  - "Remote work has benefits and costs that both extreme policies overlook."
  - "Remote work should be abolished."
  - "Offices are no longer necessary."
  - "Managers have always supported remote work."
respuesta: "Remote work has benefits and costs that both extreme policies overlook."
tipo: mc
explicacion: "El texto matiza: mejoras individuales, pérdida de colaboración espontánea."
```

metadata:
  materia: "idiomas-extranjeros/ingles"
  tema: "examen-jefe"
  nivel: "C1"
  tags: ["reading", "examen-jefe"]
pasos:
  - "Leer con atención y elegir la opción correcta."
enunciado: "Texto: «The novelist's early success was, in his own words, a mixed blessing. Critics praised the debut's unflinching realism, but every subsequent book was measured against it, and each was found wanting. It was only when he abandoned the gritty urban settings that had made his name, and turned to gentle comedies of village life, that he found a new readership. Ironically, the reviewers who once dismissed this later work as lightweight now regard it as his most subversive.» Why does the writer call the early success 'a mixed blessing'?"
opciones_explicitas:
  - "It made him poor."
  - "Critics hated the debut."
  - "Later books were unfavourably compared with it."
  - "He wrote too quickly."
respuesta: "Later books were unfavourably compared with it."
tipo: mc
explicacion: "Cada libro posterior se medía contra el debut."
```

metadata:
  materia: "idiomas-extranjeros/ingles"
  tema: "examen-jefe"
  nivel: "C1"
  tags: ["reading", "examen-jefe"]
pasos:

enunciado: "Texto: «Cities are often described as if they were machines, with roads as arteries and districts as organs. Urban planners have long been seduced by this metaphor, which promises predictability. But a city behaves less like a machine than like an ecosystem: its parts adapt, compete and cooperate in ways no blueprint anticipates. The most successful neighbourhoods, research suggests, are those planned lightly, leaving space for residents to improvise.» What is the writer's view of the machine metaphor?"
opciones_explicitas:
  - "It is completely accurate."
  - "It is a recent invention."
  - "It is used by residents."
  - "It is appealing but misleading."
respuesta: "It is appealing but misleading."
tipo: mc
explicacion: "Los planificadores se sienten seducidos, pero la ciudad es más un ecosistema."
```

metadata:

  tema: "examen-jefe"
  nivel: "C1"
  tags: ["reading", "examen-jefe"]

  - "Leer con atención y elegir la opción correcta."
enunciado: "Texto: «Sleep researchers have long warned that adolescents are chronically underslept, but the usual advice, simply to go to bed earlier, ignores biology. During puberty the body clock shifts, making it physiologically hard to feel tired before eleven. Some schools that delayed their start time by an hour reported improved attendance and fewer accidents among students driving to school. Critics worry about disrupted timetables, yet the evidence suggests the disruption is modest compared with the benefits.» Why does the writer dismiss the advice to go to bed earlier?"
opciones_explicitas:
  - "Teenagers refuse to obey."
  - "It ignores changes in the body clock."
  - "It is too expensive."
  - "Sleep is unimportant."
respuesta: "It ignores changes in the body clock."
tipo: mc
explicacion: "El reloj biológico cambia en la pubertad."
```

enunciado: "Texto: «The scientist's warning was blunt: without dramatic cuts to emissions within a decade, several coastal cities will face regular flooding. Politicians, she said, treat long-term risks as somebody else's problem. Yet her tone was not despairing. She pointed to rapid falls in the cost of renewable energy and argued that, for the first time, the transition is economically attractive as well as morally necessary.» 'Blunt' means"
opciones_explicitas:
  - "vague"
  - "polite"
  - "direct and unsoftened"
  - "quiet"
respuesta: "direct and unsoftened"
tipo: mc
explicacion: "Blunt = franco, sin rodeos."
```

metadata:
  materia: "idiomas-extranjeros/ingles"
  tema: "examen-jefe"
  nivel: "C1"
  tags: ["reading", "examen-jefe"]
pasos:
  - "Leer con atención y elegir la opción correcta."
enunciado: "Texto: «Translators often say that the hardest words to translate are the small ones. A grand abstract noun can be matched, or at least explained; a modal particle, an untranslatable shrug of a word, carries a whole attitude that vanishes in the transfer. Good translation, then, is less about replacing words than about rebuilding an effect, and it inevitably involves choices that no dictionary can settle.» What is the writer's main claim?"
opciones_explicitas:
  - "Translation often means recreating an effect, not swapping words."
  - "Dictionaries settle every choice."
  - "Abstract nouns are untranslatable."
  - "Small words are unimportant."
respuesta: "Translation often means recreating an effect, not swapping words."
tipo: mc
explicacion: "Traducir es reconstruir un efecto."
```

Using tracking collars, researchers found that foxes in the suburbs travelled less than their rural cousins, having no need to range far for food, and that their numbers depended more on human waste disposal than on green space. The findings complicate efforts to manage populations, since removing bins may prove more effective than culling.» What did the study find about suburban foxes?"

  - "They avoid bins."
  - "They are declining."

Curators have replaced glass cases with replicas that can be handled, arguing that physical contact deepens understanding. metadata:

  nivel: "C1"

enunciado: "Texto: «The tortoise, so often a symbol of slowness, has recently become a symbol of resilience. Long-term studies of one island population show that individuals survive droughts by drastically reducing activity, storing water in their bodies and waiting. Biologists caution against romanticising this strategy: it works only where habitat remains intact. Once fences and roads fragment the landscape, waiting is no longer an option, because the water sources lie beyond reach.» What has the tortoise recently come to symbolise?"
opciones_explicitas:
  - "resilience"
  - "speed"
  - "fragility"
  - "aggression"
respuesta: "resilience"
tipo: mc
explicacion: "De lentitud pasó a resiliencia."
```

metadata:

  tema: "examen-jefe"
  nivel: "C1"

pasos:
  - "Leer con atención y elegir la opción correcta."
enunciado: "Texto: «Innovation is usually pictured as the flash of a lone genius. Historians of science tend to disagree. Most breakthroughs, they argue, emerge from networks: rival labs publish partial results, instrument makers refine tools, and journalists spread ideas across disciplinary boundaries. The 'genius' narrative persists because it is easier to tell, not because it is true, and it flatters prizes that can be awarded only to individuals.» What do historians of science argue?"
opciones_explicitas:
  - "Geniuses are essential."
  - "Breakthroughs come from networks."
  - "Prizes are fair."
  - "Journalists slow progress."
respuesta: "Breakthroughs come from networks."
tipo: mc
explicacion: "Las innovaciones surgen de redes."
```

enunciado: "Texto: «Innovation is usually pictured as the flash of a lone genius. Historians of science tend to disagree. Most breakthroughs, they argue, emerge from networks: rival labs publish partial results, instrument makers refine tools, and journalists spread ideas across disciplinary boundaries. The 'genius' narrative persists because it is easier to tell, not because it is true, and it flatters prizes that can be awarded only to individuals.» Why does the 'genius' narrative persist, according to the writer?"
opciones_explicitas:
  - "It is accurate."
  - "Historians support it."
  - "It is easier to tell."
  - "It is new."
respuesta: "It is easier to tell."
tipo: mc
explicacion: "Es más fácil de contar."
```

metadata:

enunciado: "Texto: «The proposal to ban cars from the city centre has divided opinion. Shopkeepers fear that customers will stay away, yet evidence from other cities is encouraging: footfall generally rises once streets become pleasant to linger in. Residents worry chiefly about access for the elderly and disabled. The council has responded by promising a shuttle service, though sceptics note that similar promises elsewhere were quietly dropped when budgets tightened.» What do shopkeepers fear?"
opciones_explicitas:
  - "Rents will rise."
  - "Cars will return."
  - "Customers will stay away."
  - "Shuttles will fail."
respuesta: "Customers will stay away."
tipo: mc
explicacion: "Temen que los clientes no vayan."
```

metadata:

  nivel: "C1"

enunciado: "Texto: «Learning a language as an adult is often framed as a race against a closing window. But recent research complicates the picture. While children typically acquire native-like pronunciation, adults are quicker at grasping grammatical rules and can outperform children in the first year. What adults lack is not aptitude but time: hours of immersion, unstructured and free of embarrassment, that children accumulate without noticing.» What does the research suggest about adult learners?"
opciones_explicitas:
  - "They cannot learn pronunciation."
  - "They are slower in all respects."
  - "They lack aptitude."
  - "They learn rules faster at first."
respuesta: "They learn rules faster at first."
tipo: mc
explicacion: "Adultos captan reglas más rápido."
```

Supporters responded that breakfast was never meant to raise scores, and that measuring it against that yardstick was unfair.» What did the rigorous evaluation find?"

  - "Big gains in scores"
  - "Lower attendance"
  - "Higher costs"
respuesta: "No improvement in test scores"
tipo: mc
explicacion: "No mejoraron las notas."

enunciado: "Texto: «Few professions have changed as swiftly as translation. Machine systems now produce fluent drafts in seconds, and many predicted the end of the human translator. Instead, demand has shifted: routine documents are machine-produced, while human experts are hired to check nuance, adapt tone and take legal responsibility for the result. Whether the profession will be reduced to editing machine output, or elevated into a consultancy role, remains an open question.» How has demand for human translators changed?"
opciones_explicitas:
  - "It has disappeared."
  - "It has shifted to checking and adapting."
  - "It has doubled."
  - "It is unchanged."
respuesta: "It has shifted to checking and adapting."
tipo: mc
explicacion: "Ahora revisan matices y asumen responsabilidad."
```

Instead of a neat lesson, it offers fragments: a childhood in a cramped flat, a failed first marriage, a decade of bureaucratic jobs. Reviewers have divided over whether this reticence is a strength or a failing. To this reader, the withholding is the point; the book trusts us to supply the meaning that lesser memoirs spell out.» What is unusual about the memoir?"
opciones_explicitas:
  - "It is very long."
  - "It is full of lessons."
  - "It avoids a triumphant story arc."
  - "It is fictional."
respuesta: "It avoids a triumphant story arc."

enunciado: "Texto: «A recent audit found that a majority of the charity's donations were spent on fundraising rather than on its stated cause. The chief executive insisted that this was a necessary investment: without sustained advertising, the flow of donations would dry up. opciones_explicitas:
  - "Donations increased."
  - "The charity closed."
  - "Fundraising failed."
  - "Most funds went on fundraising."
respuesta: "Most funds went on fundraising."
tipo: mc
explicacion: "La mayoría se gastó en recaudar fondos."

Visibility fell to a few metres, and the wind erased their footprints almost as soon as they made them. The leader, who had crossed the range twice before, reckoned they could still make the summit; her deputy argued for turning back. In the end, she yielded, later saying that the hardest decision in the mountains is the one that feels like failure.» What did the leader initially think?"

  - "They should return."
  - "They were lost."
  - "The weather was improving."
respuesta: "They could reach the summit."

metadata:
  materia: "idiomas-extranjeros/ingles"
  tema: "examen-jefe"
  nivel: "C1"
  tags: ["reading", "examen-jefe"]
pasos:
  - "Leer con atención y elegir la opción correcta."
enunciado: "Texto: «It is tempting to see the decline of handwriting as a straightforward loss. Yet cognitive scientists remind us that writing has always adapted to its tools; the move from quill to ballpoint provoked similar lament. What matters, they suggest, is not the instrument but the depth of attention it encourages. Preliminary studies indicate that students taking notes by hand process material more deeply, though the effect appears to diminish once typists learn to paraphrase rather than transcribe.» What do cognitive scientists remind us?"
opciones_explicitas:
  - "Handwriting is superior."
  - "Writing has always adapted to tools."
  - "Quills were better."
  - "Typing is harmful."
respuesta: "Writing has always adapted to tools."
tipo: mc
explicacion: "Escribir siempre se adaptó a sus herramientas."
```

Homeowners on the eastern shore, some of whom have watched their gardens vanish over the cliff edge, are asking who should pay for defences. The government insists that individuals must bear the risk of living by the sea; residents reply that decades of planning permission, granted by that same government, implied otherwise. A compensation scheme, if introduced, would set a precedent with enormous consequences.» What is new about coastal erosion?"

  - "its existence"
  - "its location"
  - "its speed"
  - "its cause"
respuesta: "its speed"
tipo: mc
explicacion: "El ritmo se ha acelerado."

metadata:
  materia: "idiomas-extranjeros/ingles"
  tema: "examen-jefe"
  nivel: "C1"
  tags: ["reading", "examen-jefe"]
pasos:
  - "Leer con atención y elegir la opción correcta."
enunciado: "Texto: «In recent years, the word 'authentic' has become ubiquitous in marketing, attached to everything from coffee to holiday destinations. Yet authenticity, by definition, resists being manufactured. The more relentlessly it is advertised, the more suspicious consumers become, which explains why some brands now emphasise imperfection: the slightly crooked label, the deliberately unpolished video. Whether this is honesty or merely a subtler form of polish is a question the consumer must judge alone.» Why do brands emphasise imperfection?"
opciones_explicitas:
  - "Costs are lower."
  - "Laws require it."
  - "Designers prefer it."
  - "Consumers distrust over-advertised authenticity."
respuesta: "Consumers distrust over-advertised authenticity."
tipo: mc
explicacion: "Buscan parecer auténticos."
```

  - "he was guilty"
  - "he expected the verdict"
respuesta: "he expected the verdict"
tipo: mc
explicacion: "Estudiar el techo indica que esperaba el veredicto."
```

```
metadata:
  materia: "idiomas-extranjeros/ingles"
  tema: "examen-jefe"
  nivel: "C1"
  tags: ["reading", "examen-jefe"]
pasos:
  - "Leer con atención y elegir la opción correcta."
enunciado: "Texto: «The trial ended not with a dramatic confession but with a slow accumulation of doubt. Each witness, when cross-examined, retreated slightly from their earlier certainty, until the prosecution's case resembled a structure whose supports had been quietly removed. The verdict, when it came, surprised nobody in the courtroom, least of all the accused, who had spent the final day studying the ceiling.» 'Least of all' means"
opciones_explicitas:
  - "especially not"
  - "partly"
  - "perhaps"
  - "first"
respuesta: "especially not"
tipo: mc
explicacion: "Least of all = y menos que nadie."
```

  - "high cost"
  - "noise"
respuesta: "lack of control"

enunciado: "You need to end a conversation politely. Which is best?"
opciones_explicitas:
  - "I go now."
  - "It's been lovely talking to you, but I must dash."
  - "Enough talk."
  - "Bye, I'm bored."
respuesta: "It's been lovely talking to you, but I must dash."
tipo: mc
explicacion: "Despedida educada."
```
